New strings: `ui.soundAsk.hintTouch` "Better with headphones · the Sound button at the bottom switches the sound on or off any time" / "Fejhallgatóval izgalmasabb · a hang az alsó Hang gombbal bármikor be- és kikapcsolható" ("alsó", as `controlsTouch` says, corrected after review from "a lenti"); `ui.hud.touchHint` "Drag to look around · push the stick to walk · tap a work to open it" / "Húzás: körülnézés · a kar eltolása: séta · koppintás egy műre: megnyitás" (since F2 and the review: "… · a kar tolása: séta · … · a Következő mű gomb: továbblépés"). `tests/a11y/hud.mjs` has six new steps: D11.1, the question on a phone in both languages and after a switch while it shows, the Sound button at the bottom, and the M words on a computer; D11.2, at 375 × 667 in both languages, 320 × 640 and 667 × 375, the touch hint shown, hidden from screen readers, not focusable, focus on the 3D view, over none of the controls named above; D11.3, touches sent through the DevTools protocol: the first touch on the view takes the hint away, a drag turns the view, the stick held still for 4 seconds does not walk and pushed walks, a tap in front of no. 5 opens it and it stays open; D11.4, switched to Hungarian while it shows, then gone by itself 8.0 s after the answer; D11.5, with reduced motion, opacity 0 and no transition at the first touch; D11.6, on a computer the hint is the old one and not hidden from screen readers. With `src/main.js`, `src/style.css` and `src/controls.js` as before, D11.1 to D11.5 fail and D11.6 passes; with only `controls.js` as before, D11.3 fails (the viewer closes again). With the change all 19 steps of the file pass. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal, the wording and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- F3: the tour caption's first line now says how far along the tour is: "3 / 17 · No. 3 · Paradise Factory". Before, it read "3 · Paradise Factory · Installation, …, 2024", with nothing to say how many works were left. Reproduced on a build of the branch at 1366 × 768 by moving the tour's own clock on to each stop: the first line of no. 1 was "1 · Wrong Data Landscape Dresden · Acrylic on canvas, 140 × 160 cm, 2023". In `src/tour.js` `_text` counts the 17 works in visiting order (`visitOrder` from `src/world/route.js`, as Page Down and the viewer do); no. 3's small screen is a stop of its own in the tour, and there the line says "3 / 17 · No. 3 · Paradise Factory" again, as the viewer counts it. On the Hungarian page the title is in a `<span lang="en">` (B6's rule, `titleLang`); the caption on screen was the one place B6 had left out. The medium, size and year, which were on the first line, are now on a line of their own under it (12 px, the HUD's muted colour, `src/style.css`); the caption follows as before. A language switch while a caption shows rebuilds it (`tour.refresh`, as before). The live region is left as it was: "No. 3, Paradise Factory. Installation, …" and the caption. The tour goes in catalogue order, so the number already says how far along it is, and each caption is read in full, so it is kept short; the "3 / 17" is for the eye. New string: `ui.hud.tourAt` "{at} / {total} · No. {no} · {title}" / "{at} / {total} · {no}. mű · {title}". `tests/a11y/hud.mjs` has four new steps: F3.1, the first line at nos. 1, 3, no. 3's screen, 4 and 17 ("1 / 17 · No. 1 · Wrong Data Landscape Dresden" … "17 / 17 · No. 17 · Natural Life"), no lang mark in English, and "Acrylic on canvas, 140 × 160 cm, 2023" on the line under no. 1's; F3.2, the live region says "No. 1, Wrong Data Landscape Dresden. Acrylic on canvas, 140 × 160 cm, 2023." and no "/ 17"; F3.3, switched to Hungarian while no. 2's caption shows: "2 / 17 · 2. mű · Human as Error VI" with the title in `<span lang="en">`, and back in English without it; F3.4, a tour started on the Hungarian page: "16 / 17 · 16. mű · Let it Flow", marked. With `src/tour.js` and `src/style.css` as before, F3.1, F3.3 and F3.4 fail and F3.2 passes; with the change all 23 steps of the file pass. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal, the wording and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- F1: the line before entering leads with the easy path, a Help button opens the controls as a panel, and a short first-use card stands by the keys once the visitor is in. Before, the title screen's line was "↑↓ or the scroll wheel to walk · ←→ to turn · drag to look · +/− to zoom · click a work to view · Shift to run", with nothing on Page Down or Enter; the full controls were only under "Behind the scenes"; inside, the toolbar had Tour, Rooms and Sound, and the only help was the hint in the middle, "Drag the mouse to look around the room." (seen on a build of the branch at 1366 × 768). (a) `ui.loader.howtoDesk` (and the English stand-in for it in `index.html`, which shows until the language file has loaded: brought into line after review, new step F1.9 in `hud.mjs` checks every such stand-in against `en.json`) is now "↑↓ walk · ←→ turn · Page Down: next work · Enter: open · drag to look" / "↑↓: séta · ←→: fordulás · Page Down: következő mű · Enter: megnyitás · húzás egérrel: körülnézés", the proposal's words; each was tried on the build: ↑ and ↓ walk, ← and → turn, Page Down from where you come in goes to no. 1, Enter opens it, a drag turns the view. The touch line is unchanged. (b) "Help", not "Keys" ("Súgó"): the panel also holds the touch controls, and a phone has no keys; the mockup says Help too. The button (`data-action="help"` in `index.html`, after Sound, a "?" hidden from screen readers, title "How to move, and all the keys") opens the panel `help` in `src/ui/panels.js` through `ui.show`, the path every panel takes: focus to Close, Tab kept inside, the page behind inert (B3), Esc or Close by keyboard back to Help, Close by mouse to the 3D view. In a window 760 px wide or less, where the navbar folds into Menu, Help is not on the toolbar but in the folded menu (`.nav-narrow`), on the title screen as well: there is no room for it beside Next work (F2) on a phone's toolbar, and the mockup's phone toolbar has none; closed from there, focus goes to Menu (C7). The panel holds the two controls texts (`panels.controlsDesk`, `panels.controlsTouch`), the one for this device first. They stay under "Behind the scenes" as well, where the accessible version also takes them from: kept where they were, not moved. Inside the exhibition the panel ends with "Show the short guide again", which closes it (focus goes back as on any close) and shows the card, or on a touch screen the touch hint of D11 for another 8 seconds. (c) The card (`#guide`) shows on a computer once the sound question is answered, never while it is open, just above the on-screen keys, and lies over none of the keys, the zoom keys, the toolbar, the room label, the map and its key at 1366 × 768, 1280 × 800, 800 × 600 and 683 × 384 (200%; there the walking keys are put away, so it stands above the zoom keys with its four key lines only, up to 480 px wide so that each stays one line: with the Hungarian next line corrected after review, "Page Down vagy a Következő mű gomb: továbblépés a következő műre", the 340 px card grew up to the room label there and was not shown). It says: "Moving around" / "Mozgás a kiállításban"; "↑ ↓: walk · ← →: turn"; "Drag with the mouse: look around"; "Page Down: the next work"; "Enter: open the work in front"; "Help: all the keys"; "Goes at the first key or click". It goes at the first key, mouse press or turn of the wheel anywhere (`src/main.js`), but not at the key that showed it (Esc answering the sound question). Nothing in it takes focus or a click, so it traps nothing and blocks nothing: a click or drag where it lies reaches the 3D view, and ↑ walks at once while it goes. The mockup's "Got it" button is left out: any key or click takes the card away, so Tab would take it away before reaching the button. It is hidden from screen readers (`aria-hidden`), so it is never read: the 3D view's name (B7), read as focus comes to the view right after the sound question, says the same keys, and the card goes at the first key anyway. On a computer it takes the place of the hint in the middle, which says the same: that hint no longer shows with it and goes with it. On a touch screen the card does not show; the touch hint (D11) is the first-use help there. In windows up to 1180 px wide the card steps aside while the line naming the work under the mouse shows in the middle. In a window under 560 px wide it keeps its four key lines only, as in a short window; and where it still does not fit, inside the window and clear of the map, its key and the room label (a computer's window at phone size, 320 × 568, or 1280 × 1024 at 400%, 320 × 256, where it stood above the top of the window), it is not shown and the hint in the middle shows as before the card; Help's "Show the short guide again" then brings back that hint (`guideFits` in `src/main.js`; found in review). New step F1.8 in `hud.mjs`: at 320 × 568 in both languages and 320 × 256 the card shows only if it fits and else the hint shows, and at 480 × 700 it fits and shows. No fade. Nothing is stored, so it shows on each visit. The 3D view's name still says all keys are in the accessible version, the first link, which is still true, so B7's string is unchanged. New and changed strings: `ui.loader.howtoDesk` (above); `ui.nav.help` and `ui.hud.help` "Help" / "Súgó"; `ui.hud.helpTitle` "How to move, and all the keys" / "Mozgás és az összes billentyű"; `ui.panels.helpGuide` "Show the short guide again" / "A rövid útmutató újbóli megjelenítése"; `ui.hud.guide.title` "Moving around" / "Mozgás a kiállításban", `.walk` "↑ ↓: walk · ← →: turn" / "↑ ↓: séta · ← →: fordulás", `.look` "Drag with the mouse: look around" / "Húzás egérrel: körülnézés", `.next` "Page Down: the next work" / "Page Down: a következő mű", `.open` "Enter: open the work in front" / "Enter: a nézet közepén lévő mű megnyitása", `.help` "Help: all the keys" / "Súgó: minden billentyű", `.close` "Goes at the first key or click" / "Az első billentyűleütésre vagy kattintásra eltűnik" (corrected after review from "Az első billentyűre …"). `tests/a11y/hud.mjs` has new steps F1.1 to F1.7: the line in both languages and each key it names; the card at the four sizes above (not during the question, then shown, hidden from the accessibility tree, no focusable, the 3D view under it, focus on the view, the middle hint hidden, over no other control); it goes at ↑ (walking starts), a mouse press, the wheel, and shows again after a reload with nothing stored but the language; Help by keyboard (4 Tabs from the view, the panel named "Help", focus on Close, `#stage` inert, the computer text first, Tab kept inside, Esc back to Help); "Show the short guide again" by keyboard (the card back, focus on Help, gone at the next Tab); Help by mouse, switched to Hungarian while open ("Súgó"), Close clicked: focus on the view; at 375 × 667 with touch, Help in the menu and not on the toolbar, the touch text first, Close tapped: focus on Menu, and its button brings the touch hint back. `tests/a11y/keyboard.mjs` has the new step 10.1, Help by keyboard from the 3D view through the panel and back. Its Tab counts change on purpose, one more each, since Help is a new stop on the toolbar: 5.1 (to Works) 12 to 13, 7.1 (back to the 3D view) 25 to 26, 8.1 (to HU) 15 to 16; its log is committed with this entry. `tests/a11y/axe.mjs` scans the Help panel too, on the title screen in each language: 38 states, 0 violations; the "inside, HUD" state now has the card. With `index.html`, `src/main.js`, `src/ui/panels.js`, `src/style.css` and the language files as before, every F1 step fails (a block that stops at a missing control now fails its steps and lets the run go on); with the change all 33 steps of the file pass, `keyboard.mjs` passes 34 of 34 and `focus-mouse.mjs` 58 of 58 with its steps unchanged. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal, the wording and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- F2: "Next work" is on the toolbar on every device, the same jump as Page Down, and the first-use card and the touch hint name it. Before, only Page Down did this, and only the controls text under "Behind the scenes" said so; a phone had no way to it at all. Reproduced on a build of the branch at 1366 × 768 and at 375 × 667 with touch: the toolbar held Tour, Rooms, Sound (and Help on a computer, F1), nothing that goes from work to work. The button (`data-action="next"` in `index.html`, between Sound and Help, a "›" hidden from screen readers, title "Next work (Page Down)") calls `stepWork(1)` in `src/main.js`, the function Page Down calls: the same jump to the next work in visiting order, counted from the work in front, and the same words in the live region ("Room 1. …" when the room changes, then the work), except where Enter would not open the work: after a mouse click focus goes to the 3D view and Enter opens it, so the message is Page Down's, "No. 1, Wrong Data Landscape Dresden. Press Enter to open."; by keyboard (Enter there presses the button again) and after a tap (a phone has no Enter key) it is `ui.announce.workNext` "No. 1, Wrong Data Landscape Dresden." / "1. mű, Wrong Data Landscape Dresden.", without "Press Enter to open" (corrected after review: at first every press said "Press Enter to open", which was not true on the button or on a phone). One jump per press or tap: a held Enter on the button repeats, and its repeats are stopped, as Page Down stops its own (WCAG 2.3.1). By keyboard focus stays on the button, so Enter goes on to the next work (`stepWork` takes `{ focus: false }` from it, and `enter: false` unless a mouse clicked it); a mouse click or a tap hands focus to the 3D view afterwards, like every toolbar button, so Enter then opens the work. No "Previous work" beside it: on a 320 px phone the four buttons fill the toolbar already, and on a computer Page Up does it; the viewer has Previous too. Three layout changes make room (`src/style.css`): up to 480 px wide, the toolbar's words go under their signs, as in the mockup, and "Next work" may take two lines ("Következő / mű"); every button stays at least 44 × 44 px on touch (55 px high), and the toolbar stays inside a 320 px window and clear of the stick; on a computer up to 1100 px wide, the toolbar stands at the right, since in the middle, with five buttons, it reached the on-screen keys (800 × 600: 44 px over the keys); Help stays in the folded menu at 760 px or less (F1). New strings: `ui.hud.next` "Next work" / "Következő mű", `ui.hud.nextTitle` "Next work (Page Down)" / "Következő mű (Page Down)", `ui.announce.workNext` "No. {no}, {title}." / "{no}. mű, {title}.". The controls texts that Help shows, "Behind the scenes" and the accessible version name the button too (added after review: Help, on a phone the place to look, left it out). `ui.panels.controlsTouch` now ends "The buttons at the bottom start the tour, list the rooms and switch the sound on or off, and Next work goes on to the next work." / "Az alsó gombokkal el lehet indítani a tárlatvezetést, meg lehet nyitni a termek listáját, be- és ki lehet kapcsolni a hangot, a Következő mű gombbal pedig tovább lehet lépni a következő műre." (before: "… start the tour, list the rooms and mute the sound." / "Az alsó gombok elindítják a tárlatvezetést, felsorolják a termeket és némítják a hangot."); `ui.panels.controlsDesk` says after Page Down and Page Up "; the Next work button on the toolbar does the same as Page Down." / "; az eszköztár Következő mű gombja ugyanazt teszi, mint a Page Down." F1.4, F1.6 and F1.7 in `hud.mjs` now also check that both texts in the Help panel name the button, in English and, after a switch while it is open, in Hungarian. Changed: `ui.hud.guide.next` "Page Down or Next work: the next work" / "Page Down vagy a Következő mű gomb: továbblépés a következő műre"; `ui.hud.touchHint` "Drag to look around · push the stick to walk · tap a work to open it · Next work: on to the next one" / "Húzás: körülnézés · a kar tolása: séta · koppintás egy műre: megnyitás · a Következő mű gomb: továbblépés" (both Hungarian lines corrected after review, from "Page Down vagy Következő mű: a következő mű" and "… a kar eltolása: séta · … · Következő mű: továbblépés", so the button reads as a button and the stick is pushed, "tolni") (D11's hint, which is the touch screen's first-use help). With the longer hint, on a phone on its side it reached the room label (667 × 375 with a longer wording first, and 640 × 360 even with D11's), so there it now stands low, between the stick and the zoom keys, just above the level of the toolbar, and no longer above the middle of the view. Under 360 px high (568 × 320) it also stays left of the map: with the Hungarian wording corrected after review it takes three lines there and reached up past the map's lower corner, so it is narrower and takes four. `tests/a11y/hud.mjs` has new steps F2.1 to F2.5: at 1366 × 768, 1280 × 800, 900 × 700, 800 × 600, 683 × 384 and on phones (375 × 667 in both languages, 320 × 640, 667 × 375) the button with its words and title, the toolbar inside the window and over none of the keys, the zoom keys, the stick, the map, its key, the room label and the card, on touch every button at least 44 px; clicked: to no. 1 and said as Page Down says it, focus on the 3D view, again to no. 2, and Enter opens no. 2; on the Hungarian page by keyboard: "1. mű, Wrong Data Landscape Dresden." said, without "Megnyitás: Enter.", focus stays on the button, Enter again to no. 2, Enter held with four repeats goes to no. 3 only and opens nothing; on a phone, tapped twice: nos. 1 and 2, said without "Press Enter to open", and the touch hint names Next work; the card's line in both languages. `tests/a11y/keyboard.mjs` has the new step 10.2: Shift+Tab from Help to Next work, Enter says the work after no. 11 ("No. 12, Artificial Paradise II.", without "Press Enter to open"), focus stays, Enter held jumps once more only. Its Tab counts change on purpose again, one more each for the new button: 5.1 13 to 14, 7.1 26 to 27, 8.1 16 to 17, 10.1 4 to 5; its log is committed with this entry. In that log step 7.2 ("Esc stops the tour") read "Tour stopped. Room 1. Paintings, Paradise Factory. No. 2, Human as Error VI. Press Enter to open." where earlier logs read "Tour stopped.": timing, not a change. Run after review on builds of b975090 and of the branch: on both builds, after the tour stops the work in front is said ("No. 2, …") when the visitor had been moving by keyboard, and the room is said when the tour has taken the visitor into another one; whether that comes in the same message as "Tour stopped." depends on when the software-drawn frame comes, so the next run read "Tour stopped." alone in 7.2 and "Room 1. …" inside 7.1. The room is said once per room, not again. With `index.html`, `src/main.js`, `src/style.css` and the language files as before, every F2 step fails; with the change all 47 steps of `hud.mjs` pass (D11.2 now also at 640 × 360, zoomed in so the zoom readout shows) and `keyboard.mjs` passes 35 of 35. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal, the wording and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- D2: on a touch screen the stick walks as soon as it is pressed and held off its middle, without a drag, and two turning keys, ← and → (44 × 44 px), show by it. Before, a touch on the stick only took note of where the finger was; it walked only once the finger moved, and all the on-screen keys were hidden on touch (`body.touch #keypad { display: none; }`), so turning needed a drag on the view. Reproduced on a build of the branch at 375 × 667 with touch: a touch held 30 px above the stick's middle for 4 seconds did not move the visitor (the old step D11.3 checked exactly that, "stick held still walked: false"), and no turning key showed. In `src/controls.js` the stick's `touchstart` now sets the direction from where the finger lands (`moveJoy`), as a move would; lifting the finger stops it, as before. In `src/style.css` the keypad shows on touch with its two turning keys only (Forward, Back, Run and the wheel line stay hidden there): upright, just above the stick; on a phone on its side (under 520 px high), where the room label leaves no room above the stick, left of the zoom keys, level with their lower key, and the touch hint there keeps clear of them (its width now leaves 174 px at each side), and the zoom readout stands above the two keys instead of in their place. The keys are the existing ones (`src/ui/keypad.js`): held by a finger they turn until it lifts, a tap gives a short turn, and their names are unchanged ("Turn left (←)", "Turn right (→)"). Measured at 375 × 667, 320 × 640, 667 × 375, 740 × 360 and 568 × 320: the keys lie over none of the stick, the toolbar, the zoom keys, the zoom readout, the map, its key, the room label and the touch hint. Left as it was, and noted for the look (H1): on a small phone on its side (568 × 320) the zoom keys already reached 12 px into the bottom of the map before this change; at 844 × 390 the zoom keys already touched the map's key. "Next work" on the phone toolbar was already there (F2) and is not added again. Changed strings, since pressing and holding is now enough: `ui.hud.touchHint` "Drag to look around · hold the stick to walk · tap a work to open it · Next work: on to the next one" / "Húzás: körülnézés · a kar nyomva tartása: séta · koppintás egy műre: megnyitás · a Következő mű gomb: továbblépés" (was "push the stick to walk" / "a kar tolása: séta"); in `ui.panels.controlsTouch`, the first sentences now read "On a touch screen, walk with the stick on the left: press and hold it forward or back to walk, or to one side to step sideways; there is no need to drag. Pushed all the way, it runs. The ← and → buttons turn, with a tap or held down." / "Érintőképernyőn a bal oldali karral lehet sétálni: előre vagy hátra nyomva tartva előre és hátra, oldalra nyomva tartva oldalra lehet lépni, húzni nem kell. A kart a széléig tolva futni lehet. A ← és a → gomb koppintásra vagy nyomva tartva balra, illetve jobbra fordít." `tests/a11y/hud.mjs`: D11.3's expectation turned round on purpose, since D2 is the change it was waiting for: a touch held 30 px above the stick's middle must now walk (it said "Only pressing and holding the stick does not walk"); new D2.1, at five phone sizes in both languages and zoomed in, only the two turning keys show, by name, each at least 44 × 44 px, inside the window and over none of the controls listed; new D2.2, the stick pressed and held above its middle walks forward with no move of the finger and stops when it lifts, and the turn-left key held by a finger turns left and lets go. Run on a build of the branch: D2.1 5 of 5, D2.2 and D11.3 pass, and the rest of `hud.mjs` as before (60 of 61 on the first run, the one failure D2.2's own first check for the stop, which waited two frames while the visitor still glides to a halt; it now waits until the position no longer changes from frame to frame). With the change taken out, D2.1 finds no turning keys on touch, and D2.2 and D11.3 find no walk from a held stick. Fixed after a review: at 568 × 320 with touch, zoomed in, the zoom readout moved above the two keys lay 12 px over the map (reproduced: readout 433,140 67 × 44, map 408,50 150 × 102). Under 360 px high it now stands at the top in the middle, under the map's key (`src/style.css`): 251,78 67 × 44, clear of the map's key (to y 74), the room label (to x 204), the map (from x 408) and the hint (from y 134 in Hungarian, 148 in English). D2.1 now also checks the zoom readout against the map, its key, the room label, the toolbar, the zoom keys, the stick and the hint; with the fix taken out it fails at 568 × 320 in both languages, checked that way. Run on a build: D2.1 at 375 × 667, 320 × 640, 667 × 375, 740 × 360 and 568 × 320 (Hungarian, and English in a separate run) pass, no console errors. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- D8: on a short screen on its side (under 520 px high, in landscape) the viewer shows the work and its text side by side: the work on the left, as high as the window allows under the navbar, and the text in a column on the right (at least 240 px, at most 40% of the width or 380 px), which scrolls by itself and starts below the Close button. Before, from 820 px wide down the viewer stacked the work over the text whatever the height, so on a phone on its side the work had 55% of a short window less its padding. Reproduced on a build of the branch with touch, no. 1 (Wrong Data Landscape Dresden) in the viewer: 167 × 146 px at 667 × 375, 158 × 138 at 740 × 360, 133 × 116 at 568 × 320; at 844 × 390 the computer layout already put them side by side, at 336 × 294. After (the same build with the change): 360 × 315 at 667 × 375, 343 × 300 at 740 × 360, 297 × 260 at 568 × 320, and 377 × 330 at 844 × 390, where the same rule now applies (smaller margins round the work); no. 5, a portrait canvas, goes from 195 × 146 to 376 × 282 at 667 × 375. The rule is one media query in `src/style.css` (`(orientation: landscape) and (max-height: 519px)`), so it also holds for a computer window that short, such as a laptop at 200% (683 × 384); upright phones and full-size windows keep their layout (375 × 667 and 1366 × 768 measured the same before and after). Videos and the Google Drive player take the same height as the picture. No new strings. Left as it was, for the look (H1): upright at 375 × 667 the Close button already reaches 8 px over the top of the work, and the work 24 px under the top of the text panel. `tests/a11y/hud.mjs` has a new step, D8.1, at 667 × 375, 740 × 360, 844 × 390 and 568 × 320 with touch (the work at least 340 × 300, 330 × 290, 360 × 320 and 280 × 250 px, the text a full-height column beside it, Close over neither the work nor the title, no sideways scroll) and at 375 × 667 (still stacked, the work at least 340 × 290 px); with the change taken out the four landscape cases fail on size and on the column. Run on a build of the branch: 5 of 5, no console errors. Fixed after a review: the rule also holds for a computer window at 400% zoom, and there the 240 px text column left the work an 80 px strip, so it was smaller than stacked before D8 (reproduced with a mouse: no. 1 at 320 × 256 was 92 × 81 before D8 and 56 × 49 after; at 341 × 192 it had grown from 52 × 46 to 77 × 67). Now, up to 480 px wide, the text column takes at most 45% (at least 140 px), with smaller side padding and an 18 px title: no. 1 is 152 × 133 at 320 × 256 and 151 × 132 at 341 × 192. D8.1 has these two sizes with a mouse, the work at least as large as before D8; with the fix taken out the 320 × 256 case fails (56 × 49), checked that way. Run on a build: D8.1 7 of 7, no console errors. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- E2: in a very small window on a computer, such as a page at 400% zoom (320 × 256 CSS px on a 1280 × 1024 screen, 341 × 192 on 1366 × 768), the controls over the 3D view are now a compact layer, as on a phone: no footer, no on-screen keys or zoom keys, no map or map key, no hint in the middle and no first-use card; the navbar, the room label (its name only, one line), the toolbar at the bottom and, when zoomed in, the zoom readout at the top right. Before, they piled up: reproduced on a build of the branch at 320 × 256 with a mouse, inside, the footer took 140 px from y 116, the toolbar stood at y 43 over the navbar, the room label and the map, the zoom keys over the toolbar and the room label, and the hint ran 16 px above the window; at 320 × 192 and 341 × 192 the toolbar and the zoom keys began above the top of the window (y −21 and −40). It is one media query in `src/style.css`, for a window up to 480 px wide and 400 px high or up to 760 px wide and 300 px high, and only where the page is not a touch screen (phones keep their own layout). The footer's four links are in the folded menu, which every window up to 760 px wide has (D3), so nothing is lost; Help is in that menu too (F1). The keyboard still walks, turns, zooms (+, −, 0) and goes to the next work with Page Down, and the 3D view's name says the keys. The rooms menu's height is set for the toolbar at the bottom. No new strings. Measured after on the same build, zoomed in: 320 × 256: navbar 0–44, room label 12,50 98 × 26, zoom readout 241,50 67 × 28, toolbar 0,183 306 × 65; 320 × 192: the toolbar at y 119; 341 × 192: the toolbar at 87,131; none over another, all inside the window. Tab from the 3D view at 320 × 256: Tour, Rooms, Sound, Next work, the zoom readout, Menu, the two language buttons, then round to the skip link and the 3D view, every stop inside the window. `tests/a11y/hud.mjs`: new E2.1 (the three sizes, in both languages, zoomed in: the seven left-out parts not shown, the four kept ones inside the window and over none of each other) and E2.2 (the Tab order above at 320 × 256, and the menu's four footer links); with the change taken out both fail on the footer and the overlaps. F1.8 at 320 × 256 changed on purpose: it expected the hint in the middle to show there when the first-use card does not; now neither shows, and `guideInfo` reports a hint that is left out (`display: none`) as "none", which its old visibility check could not see. Run on a build of the branch: E2.1 3 of 3, E2.2 and F1.8 4 of 4 pass, no console errors. Fixed after a review: with the card left out, `guideFits()` in `src/main.js` measured its empty box at 0,0 as fitting, so the body was marked as showing the card (`guiding`) while nothing showed, and Help's "Show the short guide again" closed the panel and showed nothing (reproduced at 320 × 256 with a mouse, entered by keyboard: `guiding` set and the card not hidden). Now a card that is not laid out does not fit, and in this layer Help leaves out that button (`src/style.css`). New step E2.3 at 320 × 256: right after entry the card is not marked as shown, and Help opens without the button; with either part taken out it fails (both were checked that way). Run on a build: E2.1 3 of 3, E2.2, E2.3, F1.1 to F1.6, F1.8 4 of 4 and F1.9 pass, no console errors. Decided by: reconstructor (to carry out the proposal); assistant (the proposal and how it is built). Not a reconstruction decision (presentation).

- H1: the look, step 1 of the direction in `docs/design/ui-ux-proposal.md` ("Direction for the look"), with the reconstructor's one change: the accent is the blue #8fa8ff in place of the green #9dff6a. Reproduced on a build of the branch, inside at 1366 × 768: the green was the accent in 12 rules in `src/style.css` (11 through the token, one written out) and in 5 places in `src/ui/minimap.js`, and three states were told by colour alone or by colour first (a pressed toolbar button only turned its icon green on a faint white tint; the room the visitor is in had a green bar and a green name; on the floor plan the room's number was green). Changed: `src/style.css` now holds the direction's tokens (`--paper`, `--ink`, `--ink-muted`, `--blue`, `--text`, `--accent` #8fa8ff and `--accent-rgb`) and the glass is rgba(11, 12, 14, 0.85) as the proposal gives it (it was rgba(14, 15, 18, 0.85), a shade lighter). Every use of the accent is the new blue. No state is told by colour alone now: a pressed toolbar button (Tour, Sound; aria-pressed) also has a 3 px bar in the accent along its foot, and Sound also changes its icon; the current room in the rooms menu (aria-current) keeps its bar and is bold, its name now light, not in the accent (the accent as text there would be 4.20:1 on a hovered row over a white wall); on the floor plan the current room's number is bold and light on the accent tint (accent text there would be 4.06:1 over white); a pressed keycap keeps a light letter, and its tint is now laid on the glass (before, the tint replaced the glass, so the letter sat on the bare 3D view); Shift (aria-pressed) as before; the language buttons are underlined when chosen, as before; the crosshair aimed at a work grows to 2.4 times its size, as before, and now has a dark edge so the blue dot reads on a white wall. The accent is not used on paper or on the pale title band anywhere (2.20:1 and 1.96:1 there); on paper the blue #072fcc is used. The mockup (`docs/design/mockup-interface.html`) has the new accent, the bar on a pressed button and a swatch that says the accent is not used on paper. The floor plan's magenta and cyan marks and its key are unchanged. No new strings. Contrast, worked out with the glass composited over pure white (worst case) and over black (text needs 4.5:1, controls and marks 3:1):

  | Colour and background | Over white | Over black | Needed | Passes |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Accent #8fa8ff text (zoom readout, tour caption's first line) on glass 0.85 | 5.76 | 8.68 | 4.5 | yes |
  | Accent on glass 0.9 (the mockup's chips) | 6.79 | 8.65 | 4.5 | yes |
